  build: .
  image: my-app
  environment:
    DATABASE_URL: mongodb://db:27017/my_app
    BETTER_AUTH_URL: ${BETTER_AUTH_URL:-http://localhost:3000}
    BETTER_AUTH_SECRET: ${BETTER_AUTH_SECRET:?set BETTER_AUTH_SECRET}

services:
  db:
    image: mongo:8
    healthcheck:
      test:
        ["CMD", "mongosh", "--quiet", "--eval", "db.runCommand({ ping: 1 }).ok"]
      interval: 2s
      timeout: 5s
      retries: 30
    ports:
      - "127.0.0.1:27017:27017"
    volumes:
      - db-data:/data/db

  migrate:
    <<: *app
    command: ["bun", "dist/migrate.mjs"]
    depends_on:
      db:
        condition: service_healthy

  app:
    <<: *app
    depends_on:
      migrate:
        condition: service_completed_successfully
    ports:
      - "3000:3000"
    restart: unless-stopped

volumes:
  db-data:

name: my-app

x-app: &app
  build: .
  image: my-app
  environment:
    DATABASE_URL: mysql://root:mysql@db:3306/my_app
    BETTER_AUTH_URL: ${BETTER_AUTH_URL:-http://localhost:3000}
    BETTER_AUTH_SECRET: ${BETTER_AUTH_SECRET:?set BETTER_AUTH_SECRET}

services:
  db:
    image: mysql:8.4
    environment:
      MYSQL_DATABASE: my_app
      MYSQL_ROOT_PASSWORD: mysql
    healthcheck:
      test:
        ["CMD", "mysqladmin", "ping", "-h", "127.0.0.1", "-uroot", "-pmysql"]
      interval: 2s
      timeout: 5s
      retries: 30
    ports:
      - "127.0.0.1:3306:3306"
    volumes:
      - db-data:/var/lib/mysql

  migrate:
    <<: *app
    command: ["node", "dist/migrate.mjs"]
    depends_on:
      db:
        condition: service_healthy

  app:
    <<: *app
    depends_on:
      migrate:
        condition: service_completed_successfully
    ports:
      - "3000:3000"
    restart: unless-stopped

volumes:
  db-data:

name: my-app

x-app: &app
  build: .
  image: my-app
  environment:
    DATABASE_URL: postgres://postgres:postgres@db:5432/my_app
    BETTER_AUTH_URL: ${BETTER_AUTH_URL:-http://localhost:3000}
    BETTER_AUTH_SECRET: ${BETTER_AUTH_SECRET:?set BETTER_AUTH_SECRET}

services:
  db:
    image: postgres:18-alpine
    environment:
      POSTGRES_DB: my_app
      POSTGRES_PASSWORD: postgres
      POSTGRES_USER: postgres
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD-SHELL", "pg_isready -U postgres -d my_app"]
      interval: 2s
      timeout: 5s
      retries: 15
    ports:
      - "127.0.0.1:5432:5432"
    volumes:
      - db-data:/var/lib/postgresql

  migrate:
    <<: *app
    command: ["bun", "dist/migrate.mjs"]
    depends_on:
      db:
        condition: service_healthy

  app:
    <<: *app
    depends_on:
      migrate:
        condition: service_completed_successfully
    ports:
      - "3000:3000"
    restart: unless-stopped

volumes:
  db-data:

name: my-app

x-app: &app
  build: .
  image: my-app
  environment:
    DATABASE_URL: postgres://postgres:postgres@db:5432/my_app
    BETTER_AUTH_URL: ${BETTER_AUTH_URL:-http://localhost:3000}
    BETTER_AUTH_SECRET: ${BETTER_AUTH_SECRET:?set BETTER_AUTH_SECRET}

services:
  db:
    image: postgres:18-alpine
    environment:
      POSTGRES_DB: my_app
      POSTGRES_PASSWORD: postgres
      POSTGRES_USER: postgres
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD-SHELL", "pg_isready -U postgres -d my_app"]
      interval: 2s
      timeout: 5s
      retries: 15
    ports:
      - "127.0.0.1:5432:5432"
    volumes:
      - db-data:/var/lib/postgresql

  migrate:
    <<: *app
    command: ["node", "dist/migrate.mjs"]
    depends_on:
      db:
        condition: service_healthy

  app:
    <<: *app
    depends_on:
      migrate:
        condition: service_completed_successfully
    ports:
      - "3000:3000"
    restart: unless-stopped

volumes:
  db-data:

x-app: &app
  build: .
  image: my-app
  environment:
    DATABASE_URL: mongodb://db:27017/my_app
    BETTER_AUTH_URL: ${BETTER_AUTH_URL:-http://localhost:3000}
    BETTER_AUTH_SECRET: ${BETTER_AUTH_SECRET:?set BETTER_AUTH_SECRET}

services:
  db:
    image: mongo:8
    healthcheck:
      test:
        ["CMD", "mongosh", "--quiet", "--eval", "db.runCommand({ ping: 1 }).ok"]
      interval: 2s
      timeout: 5s
      retries: 30
    ports:
      - "127.0.0.1:27017:27017"
    volumes:
      - db-data:/data/db

  migrate:
    <<: *app
    command: ["bun", "dist/migrate.mjs"]
    depends_on:
      db:
        condition: service_healthy

  server:
    <<: *app
    command: ["bun", "dist/index.mjs"]
    depends_on:
      migrate:
        condition: service_completed_successfully
    restart: unless-stopped

  app:
    <<: *app
    environment:
      SERVER_URL: http://server:3000
    depends_on:
      server:
        condition: service_healthy
    ports:
      - "3000:3000"
    restart: unless-stopped

volumes:
  db-data:

name: my-app

x-app: &app
  build: .
  image: my-app
  environment:
    DATABASE_URL: mysql://root:mysql@db:3306/my_app
    BETTER_AUTH_URL: ${BETTER_AUTH_URL:-http://localhost:3000}
    BETTER_AUTH_SECRET: ${BETTER_AUTH_SECRET:?set BETTER_AUTH_SECRET}

services:
  db:
    image: mysql:8.4
    environment:
      MYSQL_DATABASE: my_app
      MYSQL_ROOT_PASSWORD: mysql
    healthcheck:
      test:
        ["CMD", "mysqladmin", "ping", "-h", "127.0.0.1", "-uroot", "-pmysql"]
      interval: 2s
      timeout: 5s
      retries: 30
    ports:
      - "127.0.0.1:3306:3306"
    volumes:
      - db-data:/var/lib/mysql

  migrate:
    <<: *app
    command: ["bun", "dist/migrate.mjs"]
    depends_on:
      db:
        condition: service_healthy

  server:
    <<: *app
    command: ["bun", "dist/index.mjs"]
    depends_on:
      migrate:
        condition: service_completed_successfully
    restart: unless-stopped

  app:
    <<: *app
    environment:
      SERVER_URL: http://server:3000
    depends_on:
      server:
        condition: service_healthy
    ports:
      - "3000:3000"
    restart: unless-stopped

volumes:
  db-data:

name: my-app

x-app: &app
  build: .
  image: my-app
  environment:
    DATABASE_URL: postgres://postgres:postgres@db:5432/my_app
    BETTER_AUTH_URL: ${BETTER_AUTH_URL:-http://localhost:3000}
    BETTER_AUTH_SECRET: ${BETTER_AUTH_SECRET:?set BETTER_AUTH_SECRET}

services:
  db:
    image: postgres:18-alpine
    environment:
      POSTGRES_DB: my_app
      POSTGRES_PASSWORD: postgres
      POSTGRES_USER: postgres
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD-SHELL", "pg_isready -U postgres -d my_app"]
      interval: 2s
      timeout: 5s
      retries: 15
    ports:
      - "127.0.0.1:5432:5432"
    volumes:
      - db-data:/var/lib/postgresql

  migrate:
    <<: *app
    command: ["bun", "dist/migrate.mjs"]
    depends_on:
      db:
        condition: service_healthy

  server:
    <<: *app
    command: ["bun", "dist/index.mjs"]
    depends_on:
      migrate:
        condition: service_completed_successfully
    restart: unless-stopped

  app:
    <<: *app
    environment:
      SERVER_URL: http://server:3000
    depends_on:
      server:
        condition: service_healthy
    ports:
      - "3000:3000"
    restart: unless-stopped

volumes:
  db-data:

name: my-app

x-app: &app
  build: .
  image: my-app
  environment:
    DATABASE_URL: postgres://postgres:postgres@db:5432/my_app
    BETTER_AUTH_URL: ${BETTER_AUTH_URL:-http://localhost:3000}
    BETTER_AUTH_SECRET: ${BETTER_AUTH_SECRET:?set BETTER_AUTH_SECRET}

services:
  db:
    image: postgres:18-alpine
    environment:
      POSTGRES_DB: my_app
      POSTGRES_PASSWORD: postgres
      POSTGRES_USER: postgres
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD-SHELL", "pg_isready -U postgres -d my_app"]
      interval: 2s
      timeout: 5s
      retries: 15
    ports:
      - "127.0.0.1:5432:5432"
    volumes:
      - db-data:/var/lib/postgresql

  migrate:
    <<: *app
    command: ["node", "dist/migrate.mjs"]
    depends_on:
      db:
        condition: service_healthy

  server:
    <<: *app
    command: ["node", "dist/index.mjs"]
    depends_on:
      migrate:
        condition: service_completed_successfully
    restart: unless-stopped

  app:
    <<: *app
    environment:
      SERVER_URL: http://server:3000
    depends_on:
      server:
        condition: service_healthy
    ports:
      - "3000:3000"
    restart: unless-stopped

volumes:
  db-data:

# Dockerfile · 1 stack
#   hono-bun-docker
# syntax=docker/dockerfile:1

FROM ghcr.io/voidzero-dev/vite-plus:1.4.0 AS build
WORKDIR /app

COPY --chown=vp:vp package.json pnpm-lock.yaml pnpm-workspace.yaml .node-version ./
COPY --chown=vp:vp apps/server/package.json apps/server/
COPY --chown=vp:vp packages/config/package.json packages/config/
RUN vp install --frozen-lockfile --filter "@my-app/server..."

COPY --chown=vp:vp . .

EXPOSE 3000

  CMD ["node", "-e", "fetch('http://127.0.0.1:3000/api/health').then((r) => process.exit(r.ok ? 0 : 1), () => process.exit(1))"]

WORKDIR /app
ENV NODE_ENV=production PORT=3000

COPY --from=build /tmp/node /usr/local/bin/node
COPY --from=build /app/apps/server/dist ./dist

USER nobody
EXPOSE 3000
HEALTHCHECK --interval=10s --timeout=3s --start-period=5s --retries=3 \
  CMD ["node", "-e", "fetch('http://127.0.0.1:3000/api/health').then((r) => process.exit(r.ok ? 0 : 1), () => process.exit(1))"]
CMD ["bun", "dist/index.mjs"]

FROM ghcr.io/voidzero-dev/vite-plus:1.4.0 AS build
WORKDIR /app

COPY --chown=vp:vp package.json pnpm-lock.yaml pnpm-workspace.yaml .node-version ./
COPY --chown=vp:vp apps/mobile/package.json apps/mobile/
COPY --chown=vp:vp apps/server/package.json apps/server/
COPY --chown=vp:vp packages/api/package.json packages/api/
COPY --chown=vp:vp packages/config/package.json packages/config/
COPY --chown=vp:vp packages/db/package.json packages/db/
RUN vp install --frozen-lockfile --filter "@my-app/server..."

COPY --chown=vp:vp . .
RUN vp run --filter "@my-app/server..." build
RUN cp "$(vp env which node | head -1)" /tmp/node

FROM oven/bun:1.4.4-slim AS runtime
RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends libatomic1 && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*
WORKDIR /app
ENV NODE_ENV=production PORT=3000 DATABASE_URL=/data/app.db

COPY --from=build /tmp/node /usr/local/bin/node
COPY --from=build /app/apps/server/dist ./dist
RUN mkdir /data && chown nobody:nogroup /data

USER nobody
VOLUME /data
EXPOSE 3000
HEALTHCHECK --interval=10s --timeout=3s --start-period=5s --retries=3 \
  CMD ["node", "-e", "fetch('http://127.0.0.1:3000/api/health').then((r) => process.exit(r.ok ? 0 : 1), () => process.exit(1))"]
CMD ["bun", "dist/index.mjs"]

FROM ghcr.io/voidzero-dev/vite-plus:1.4.0 AS build
WORKDIR /app

COPY --chown=vp:vp package.json pnpm-lock.yaml pnpm-workspace.yaml .node-version ./
COPY --chown=vp:vp apps/web/package.json apps/web/
COPY --chown=vp:vp packages/config/package.json packages/config/
COPY --chown=vp:vp packages/ui/package.json packages/ui/
RUN vp install --frozen-lockfile --filter "@my-app/web..."

COPY --chown=vp:vp . .
RUN vp run --filter "@my-app/web..." build
RUN cp "$(vp env which node | head -1)" /tmp/node

FROM debian:trixie-slim AS runtime
RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends libatomic1 && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*
WORKDIR /app
ENV NODE_ENV=production PORT=3000 HOSTNAME=0.0.0.0

COPY --from=build /tmp/node /usr/local/bin/node
COPY --from=build /app/apps/web/.next/standalone ./
COPY --from=build /app/apps/web/.next/static ./apps/web/.next/static

USER nobody
EXPOSE 3000
HEALTHCHECK --interval=10s --timeout=3s --start-period=5s --retries=3 \
  CMD ["node", "-e", "fetch('http://127.0.0.1:3000/').then((r) => process.exit(r.ok ? 0 : 1), () => process.exit(1))"]
CMD ["node", "apps/web/server.js"]

COPY --chown=vp:vp apps/mobile/package.json apps/mobile/
COPY --chown=vp:vp apps/server/package.json apps/server/
COPY --chown=vp:vp apps/web/package.json apps/web/

COPY --chown=vp:vp packages/config/package.json packages/config/

RUN vp install --frozen-lockfile --filter "@my-app/server..." --filter "@my-app/web..."

CMD ["node", "apps/web/server.js"]

#   next-hono-openapi-mysql-prisma-expo-docker-e2e
#   next-hono-openapi-postgres-expo-docker
#   next-hono-openapi-postgres-expo-docker-e2e
#   next-hono-openapi-postgres-prisma-expo-docker
#   next-hono-openapi-postgres-prisma-expo-docker-e2e
#   next-hono-orpc-mongodb-expo-docker
#   next-hono-orpc-mongodb-expo-docker-e2e

#   next-hono-orpc-mysql-expo-docker-e2e
#   next-hono-orpc-mysql-prisma-expo-docker
#   next-hono-orpc-mysql-prisma-expo-docker-e2e
#   next-hono-orpc-postgres-expo-docker
#   next-hono-orpc-postgres-expo-docker-e2e
#   next-hono-orpc-postgres-prisma-expo-docker
#   next-hono-orpc-postgres-prisma-expo-docker-e2e
# syntax=docker/dockerfile:1

FROM ghcr.io/voidzero-dev/vite-plus:1.4.0 AS build
WORKDIR /app

COPY --chown=vp:vp package.json pnpm-lock.yaml pnpm-workspace.yaml .node-version ./
COPY --chown=vp:vp apps/mobile/package.json apps/mobile/
COPY --chown=vp:vp apps/server/package.json apps/server/
COPY --chown=vp:vp apps/web/package.json apps/web/
COPY --chown=vp:vp packages/api/package.json packages/api/
COPY --chown=vp:vp packages/config/package.json packages/config/
COPY --chown=vp:vp packages/db/package.json packages/db/
COPY --chown=vp:vp packages/ui/package.json packages/ui/
RUN vp install --frozen-lockfile --filter "@my-app/server..." --filter "@my-app/web..."

COPY --chown=vp:vp . .
RUN vp run --filter "@my-app/server..." --filter "@my-app/web..." build
RUN cp "$(vp env which node | head -1)" /tmp/node

FROM debian:trixie-slim AS runtime
RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends libatomic1 && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*
WORKDIR /app
ENV NODE_ENV=production PORT=3000 HOSTNAME=0.0.0.0

COPY --from=build /tmp/node /usr/local/bin/node
COPY --from=build /app/apps/web/.next/standalone ./
COPY --from=build /app/apps/web/.next/static ./apps/web/.next/static
COPY --from=build /app/apps/server/dist ./dist

USER nobody
EXPOSE 3000
HEALTHCHECK --interval=10s --timeout=3s --start-period=5s --retries=3 \
  CMD ["node", "-e", "fetch('http://127.0.0.1:3000/api/health').then((r) => process.exit(r.ok ? 0 : 1), () => process.exit(1))"]
CMD ["node", "apps/web/server.js"]

# Dockerfile · 2 stacks
#   spa-docker
#   spa-docker-e2e
# syntax=docker/dockerfile:1

FROM ghcr.io/voidzero-dev/vite-plus:1.4.0 AS build
WORKDIR /app

COPY --chown=vp:vp package.json pnpm-lock.yaml pnpm-workspace.yaml .node-version ./
COPY --chown=vp:vp apps/web/package.json apps/web/

RUN vp run --filter "@my-app/web..." build

FROM nginxinc/nginx-unprivileged:1.30-alpine AS runtime
COPY <<'EOF' /etc/nginx/conf.d/default.conf
server {
  listen 3000;
  root /usr/share/nginx/html;
  location / {
    try_files $uri $uri/ /index.html;
  }
}
EOF
COPY --from=build /app/apps/web/dist /usr/share/nginx/html

EXPOSE 3000
HEALTHCHECK --interval=10s --timeout=3s --start-period=5s --retries=3 \
  CMD wget --quiet --spider http://127.0.0.1:3000/ || exit 1

# Dockerfile · 2 stacks
#   spa-electron-docker
#   spa-electron-docker-e2e
# syntax=docker/dockerfile:1

FROM ghcr.io/voidzero-dev/vite-plus:1.4.0 AS build
WORKDIR /app

COPY --chown=vp:vp package.json pnpm-lock.yaml pnpm-workspace.yaml .node-version ./
COPY --chown=vp:vp apps/desktop/package.json apps/desktop/
COPY --chown=vp:vp apps/web/package.json apps/web/
COPY --chown=vp:vp packages/config/package.json packages/config/
COPY --chown=vp:vp packages/electron/package.json packages/electron/
COPY --chown=vp:vp packages/ui/package.json packages/ui/
RUN vp install --frozen-lockfile --filter "@my-app/web..."

COPY --chown=vp:vp . .
RUN vp run --filter "@my-app/web..." build

FROM nginxinc/nginx-unprivileged:1.30-alpine AS runtime
COPY <<'EOF' /etc/nginx/conf.d/default.conf
server {
  listen 3000;
  root /usr/share/nginx/html;
  location / {
    try_files $uri $uri/ /index.html;
  }
}
EOF
COPY --from=build /app/apps/web/dist /usr/share/nginx/html

EXPOSE 3000
HEALTHCHECK --interval=10s --timeout=3s --start-period=5s --retries=3 \
  CMD wget --quiet --spider http://127.0.0.1:3000/ || exit 1

# syntax=docker/dockerfile:1

FROM ghcr.io/voidzero-dev/vite-plus:1.4.0 AS build

# Dockerfile · 2 stacks
#   spa-hono-bun-docker
#   spa-hono-bun-docker-e2e
# syntax=docker/dockerfile:1

FROM ghcr.io/voidzero-dev/vite-plus:1.4.0 AS build
WORKDIR /app

COPY --chown=vp:vp package.json pnpm-lock.yaml pnpm-workspace.yaml .node-version ./
COPY --chown=vp:vp apps/server/package.json apps/server/
COPY --chown=vp:vp apps/web/package.json apps/web/
COPY --chown=vp:vp packages/config/package.json packages/config/
COPY --chown=vp:vp packages/ui/package.json packages/ui/
RUN vp install --frozen-lockfile --filter "@my-app/server..." --filter "@my-app/web..."

COPY --chown=vp:vp . .
RUN vp run --filter "@my-app/server..." --filter "@my-app/web..." build
RUN cp "$(vp env which node | head -1)" /tmp/node

FROM oven/bun:1.4.4-slim AS runtime
RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends libatomic1 && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*
WORKDIR /app
ENV NODE_ENV=production PORT=3000

COPY --from=build /tmp/node /usr/local/bin/node
COPY --from=build /app/apps/server/dist ./dist
COPY --from=build /app/apps/web/dist ./dist/public

USER nobody
EXPOSE 3000
HEALTHCHECK --interval=10s --timeout=3s --start-period=5s --retries=3 \
  CMD ["node", "-e", "fetch('http://127.0.0.1:3000/api/health').then((r) => process.exit(r.ok ? 0 : 1), () => process.exit(1))"]
CMD ["bun", "dist/index.mjs"]

#   spa-hono-mysql-prisma-better-auth-electron-docker-e2e
#   spa-hono-postgres-better-auth-electron-docker
#   spa-hono-postgres-better-auth-electron-docker-e2e
#   spa-hono-postgres-prisma-better-auth-electron-docker
#   spa-hono-postgres-prisma-better-auth-electron-docker-e2e
# syntax=docker/dockerfile:1

FROM ghcr.io/voidzero-dev/vite-plus:1.4.0 AS build
WORKDIR /app

COPY --chown=vp:vp package.json pnpm-lock.yaml pnpm-workspace.yaml .node-version ./
COPY --chown=vp:vp apps/desktop/package.json apps/desktop/
COPY --chown=vp:vp apps/server/package.json apps/server/
COPY --chown=vp:vp apps/web/package.json apps/web/
COPY --chown=vp:vp packages/auth/package.json packages/auth/
COPY --chown=vp:vp packages/config/package.json packages/config/
COPY --chown=vp:vp packages/db/package.json packages/db/
COPY --chown=vp:vp packages/electron/package.json packages/electron/
COPY --chown=vp:vp packages/ui/package.json packages/ui/
RUN vp install --frozen-lockfile --filter "@my-app/server..." --filter "@my-app/web..."

COPY --chown=vp:vp . .
RUN vp run --filter "@my-app/server..." --filter "@my-app/web..." build
RUN cp "$(vp env which node | head -1)" /tmp/node

FROM debian:trixie-slim AS runtime
RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends libatomic1 && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*
WORKDIR /app
ENV NODE_ENV=production PORT=3000

COPY --from=build /tmp/node /usr/local/bin/node
COPY --from=build /app/apps/server/dist ./dist
COPY --from=build /app/apps/web/dist ./dist/public

USER nobody
EXPOSE 3000
HEALTHCHECK --interval=10s --timeout=3s --start-period=5s --retries=3 \
  CMD ["node", "-e", "fetch('http://127.0.0.1:3000/api/health').then((r) => process.exit(r.ok ? 0 : 1), () => process.exit(1))"]
CMD ["node", "dist/index.mjs"]

#   spa-hono-orpc-mysql-prisma-better-auth-docker
#   spa-hono-orpc-mysql-prisma-better-auth-docker-e2e
#   spa-hono-orpc-postgres-better-auth-docker
#   spa-hono-orpc-postgres-better-auth-docker-e2e
#   spa-hono-orpc-postgres-prisma-better-auth-docker
#   spa-hono-orpc-postgres-prisma-better-auth-docker-e2e
# syntax=docker/dockerfile:1

FROM ghcr.io/voidzero-dev/vite-plus:1.4.0 AS build
WORKDIR /app

COPY --chown=vp:vp package.json pnpm-lock.yaml pnpm-workspace.yaml .node-version ./
COPY --chown=vp:vp apps/server/package.json apps/server/
COPY --chown=vp:vp apps/web/package.json apps/web/
COPY --chown=vp:vp packages/api/package.json packages/api/
COPY --chown=vp:vp packages/auth/package.json packages/auth/
COPY --chown=vp:vp packages/config/package.json packages/config/
COPY --chown=vp:vp packages/db/package.json packages/db/
COPY --chown=vp:vp packages/ui/package.json packages/ui/
RUN vp install --frozen-lockfile --filter "@my-app/server..." --filter "@my-app/web..."

COPY --chown=vp:vp . .
RUN vp run --filter "@my-app/server..." --filter "@my-app/web..." build
RUN cp "$(vp env which node | head -1)" /tmp/node

FROM debian:trixie-slim AS runtime
RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends libatomic1 && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*
WORKDIR /app
ENV NODE_ENV=production PORT=3000

COPY --from=build /tmp/node /usr/local/bin/node
COPY --from=build /app/apps/server/dist ./dist
COPY --from=build /app/apps/web/dist ./dist/public

USER nobody
EXPOSE 3000
HEALTHCHECK --interval=10s --timeout=3s --start-period=5s --retries=3 \
  CMD ["node", "-e", "fetch('http://127.0.0.1:3000/api/health').then((r) => process.exit(r.ok ? 0 : 1), () => process.exit(1))"]
CMD ["node", "dist/index.mjs"]

#   spa-hono-orpc-sqlite-electron-expo-bun-docker
#   spa-hono-orpc-sqlite-electron-expo-bun-docker-e2e
# syntax=docker/dockerfile:1

FROM ghcr.io/voidzero-dev/vite-plus:1.4.0 AS build
WORKDIR /app

COPY --chown=vp:vp package.json pnpm-lock.yaml pnpm-workspace.yaml .node-version ./
COPY --chown=vp:vp apps/desktop/package.json apps/desktop/
COPY --chown=vp:vp apps/mobile/package.json apps/mobile/
COPY --chown=vp:vp apps/server/package.json apps/server/
COPY --chown=vp:vp apps/web/package.json apps/web/
COPY --chown=vp:vp packages/api/package.json packages/api/
COPY --chown=vp:vp packages/config/package.json packages/config/
COPY --chown=vp:vp packages/db/package.json packages/db/
COPY --chown=vp:vp packages/electron/package.json packages/electron/
COPY --chown=vp:vp packages/ui/package.json packages/ui/
RUN vp install --frozen-lockfile --filter "@my-app/server..." --filter "@my-app/web..."

COPY --chown=vp:vp . .
RUN vp run --filter "@my-app/server..." --filter "@my-app/web..." build
RUN cp "$(vp env which node | head -1)" /tmp/node

FROM oven/bun:1.4.4-slim AS runtime
RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends libatomic1 && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*
WORKDIR /app
ENV NODE_ENV=production PORT=3000 DATABASE_URL=/data/app.db

COPY --from=build /tmp/node /usr/local/bin/node
COPY --from=build /app/apps/server/dist ./dist
COPY --from=build /app/apps/web/dist ./dist/public
RUN mkdir /data && chown nobody:nogroup /data

USER nobody
VOLUME /data
EXPOSE 3000
HEALTHCHECK --interval=10s --timeout=3s --start-period=5s --retries=3 \
  CMD ["node", "-e", "fetch('http://127.0.0.1:3000/api/health').then((r) => process.exit(r.ok ? 0 : 1), () => process.exit(1))"]
CMD ["bun", "dist/index.mjs"]

#   tanstack-start-hono-orpc-postgres-prisma-better-auth-bun-docker-e2e
# syntax=docker/dockerfile:1

FROM ghcr.io/voidzero-dev/vite-plus:1.4.0 AS build
WORKDIR /app

COPY --chown=vp:vp package.json pnpm-lock.yaml pnpm-workspace.yaml .node-version ./
COPY --chown=vp:vp apps/server/package.json apps/server/
COPY --chown=vp:vp apps/web/package.json apps/web/
COPY --chown=vp:vp packages/api/package.json packages/api/
COPY --chown=vp:vp packages/auth/package.json packages/auth/
COPY --chown=vp:vp packages/config/package.json packages/config/
COPY --chown=vp:vp packages/db/package.json packages/db/
COPY --chown=vp:vp packages/ui/package.json packages/ui/
RUN vp install --frozen-lockfile --filter "@my-app/server..." --filter "@my-app/web..."

COPY --chown=vp:vp . .
RUN vp run --filter "@my-app/server..." --filter "@my-app/web..." build
RUN cp "$(vp env which node | head -1)" /tmp/node

FROM oven/bun:1.4.4-slim AS runtime
RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends libatomic1 && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*
WORKDIR /app
ENV NODE_ENV=production PORT=3000

COPY --from=build /tmp/node /usr/local/bin/node
COPY --from=build /app/apps/web/.output ./.output
COPY --from=build /app/apps/server/dist ./dist

USER nobody
EXPOSE 3000
HEALTHCHECK --interval=10s --timeout=3s --start-period=5s --retries=3 \
  CMD ["node", "-e", "fetch('http://127.0.0.1:3000/api/health').then((r) => process.exit(r.ok ? 0 : 1), () => process.exit(1))"]
CMD ["node", ".output/server/index.mjs"]

RUN cp "$(vp env which node | head -1)" /tmp/node

FROM debian:trixie-slim AS runtime

WORKDIR /app

RUN mkdir /data && chown nobody:nogroup /data

VOLUME /data

CMD ["node", ".output/server/index.mjs"]

#   tanstack-start-self-orpc-mysql-prisma-docker-e2e
#   tanstack-start-self-orpc-postgres-docker
#   tanstack-start-self-orpc-postgres-docker-e2e
#   tanstack-start-self-orpc-postgres-prisma-docker
#   tanstack-start-self-orpc-postgres-prisma-docker-e2e
# syntax=docker/dockerfile:1

FROM ghcr.io/voidzero-dev/vite-plus:1.4.0 AS build
WORKDIR /app

COPY --chown=vp:vp package.json pnpm-lock.yaml pnpm-workspace.yaml .node-version ./
COPY --chown=vp:vp apps/web/package.json apps/web/
COPY --chown=vp:vp packages/api/package.json packages/api/
COPY --chown=vp:vp packages/config/package.json packages/config/
COPY --chown=vp:vp packages/db/package.json packages/db/
COPY --chown=vp:vp packages/ui/package.json packages/ui/
RUN vp install --frozen-lockfile --filter "@my-app/web..."

COPY --chown=vp:vp . .
RUN vp run --filter "@my-app/web..." build
RUN cp "$(vp env which node | head -1)" /tmp/node

FROM debian:trixie-slim AS runtime
RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends libatomic1 && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*
WORKDIR /app
ENV NODE_ENV=production PORT=3000

COPY --from=build /tmp/node /usr/local/bin/node
COPY --from=build /app/apps/web/.output ./.output
COPY --from=build /app/apps/web/dist ./dist

USER nobody
EXPOSE 3000
HEALTHCHECK --interval=10s --timeout=3s --start-period=5s --retries=3 \
  CMD ["node", "-e", "fetch('http://127.0.0.1:3000/api/health').then((r) => process.exit(r.ok ? 0 : 1), () => process.exit(1))"]
CMD ["node", ".output/server/index.mjs"]

# Dockerfile · 2 stacks

#   tanstack-start-self-orpc-sqlite-better-auth-docker-e2e
# syntax=docker/dockerfile:1

FROM ghcr.io/voidzero-dev/vite-plus:1.4.0 AS build
WORKDIR /app

COPY --chown=vp:vp package.json pnpm-lock.yaml pnpm-workspace.yaml .node-version ./
COPY --chown=vp:vp apps/web/package.json apps/web/
COPY --chown=vp:vp packages/api/package.json packages/api/
COPY --chown=vp:vp packages/auth/package.json packages/auth/
COPY --chown=vp:vp packages/config/package.json packages/config/
COPY --chown=vp:vp packages/db/package.json packages/db/
COPY --chown=vp:vp packages/ui/package.json packages/ui/
RUN vp install --frozen-lockfile --filter "@my-app/web..."

COPY --chown=vp:vp . .
RUN vp run --filter "@my-app/web..." build
RUN cp "$(vp env which node | head -1)" /tmp/node

FROM debian:trixie-slim AS runtime
RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends libatomic1 && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*
WORKDIR /app
ENV NODE_ENV=production PORT=3000 DATABASE_URL=/data/app.db

COPY --from=build /tmp/node /usr/local/bin/node
COPY --from=build /app/apps/web/.output ./.output
COPY --from=build /app/apps/web/dist ./dist
RUN mkdir /data && chown nobody:nogroup /data

USER nobody
VOLUME /data
EXPOSE 3000
HEALTHCHECK --interval=10s --timeout=3s --start-period=5s --retries=3 \
  CMD ["node", "-e", "fetch('http://127.0.0.1:3000/api/health').then((r) => process.exit(r.ok ? 0 : 1), () => process.exit(1))"]
CMD ["node", ".output/server/index.mjs"]
